Source: d:\BuhnetAll\SYSAPPPRJ\Zvit_buh1C\zvit_buh29_1_edit202102.SCT (FileShare.ReadWrite OK)

Q1) Where is T22RXXXXG8 written?
- Current xmlpart2 for T22 (prkasmetod=0) detail loop writes G311/G312/G32/G4/G5/G6 only — NO RXXXXG7/G8.
- Active '<T'+key2+'RXXXXG8' emitters:
  (a) PROCEDURE xmlpart — always (used for LL* tables, not T22)
  (b) PROCEDURE xmlpart2 — only inside IF prkasmetod>0 (T21 path), from TextRC(i,48)
  (c) commented * / *!* historical blocks
- No literal string 'T22RXXXXG8' in SCT. If an export contains T22RXXXXG8, it is not from current xmlpart2 T22 branch.

Q2) Command5.Click (FULL): Excel export only.
  PUTFILE(...XLS) + F1.write(cFilePath,11) by ActivePage grid. Does NOT call page2toxml/Xmlpart2.
- XML export is Command6.Click -> page2toxml() (when ActivePage=2) -> Xmlpart2('T21'/'T22').

Q3) T22END marker / fills (during report CALC, not loaddata):
  T21END: col38=lnSunR4, col43=lnSunR5, col48=lnSunR7 (+ subrows)
  T22END: col31=lnSunR4, col38=lnSunR5, col46=lnSunR7 (+ subrows on 31/38/46)
  T22END does NOT write cols 43 or 48.
  loaddata: header HZY month->col43, names, etc.; does NOT recompute T22END sums.
  Opening saved report: grid restored from saved .vts/XMLDATA; T22END 43/48 stay whatever was saved (normally blank).

Q4) Empty-tag strip: No STRTRAN(_xml...) post-process found that removes <R022G8></R022G8>.
  xmlpart uses xsi:nil when EMPTY for its G7/G8. Kushnir block has no EMPTY/nil guard.

Q5) xmlpart2 location: form method source in SCT only (also edit202304.SCT).
  Not in .prg/.fxp/.vcx. Companion .scx is metadata only (no method body).

VERDICT:
- Open existing month + press Command5 without recalc: Command5 never touches cols 43/48 and never builds XML. T22END 43/48 remain empty (calc never writes them; only T21END uses 43/48).
- R022G7/G8 completely absent: Kushnir IF prkasmetod=0 lives in xmlpart2, which runs only via Command6/page2toxml — not Command5. If XML was built without that button (or with unrecompiled methods older than 12/06/2026), tags never appear. Even when Kushnir runs, it reads T22END cols 43/48 which the T22 calc leaves empty (values are in 31/38/46).

LOCAL cFilePath, nType, oFile, cNameFile

LOCAL oErr &&AS Exception


cFilePath = ""
nType = 11
cNameFile = ""
	LOCAL F1, namef
	DO CASE
	CASE thisform.pf.ActivePage = 1
		F1 = thisform.PF.Page1.F1_1
	CASE thisform.pf.ActivePage = 2
		F1 = thisform.PF.Page5.F1_55
		cNameFile = thisform.PF.Page2.Caption 
	CASE thisform.pf.ActivePage = 3
		F1 = thisform.PF.Page3.F1_3
		cNameFile = thisform.PF.Page3.Caption 
	CASE thisform.pf.ActivePage = 4
		F1 = thisform.PF.Page4.F1_4
		cNameFile = thisform.PF.Page4.Caption 
	CASE thisform.pf.ActivePage = 5
		F1 = thisform.PF.Page5.F1_55
		cNameFile = thisform.PF.Page5.Caption 
				
	OTHERWISE

	ENDCASE

cFilePath = PUTFILE("Збереження звіту...",IIF(EMPTY(cNameFile),"Zvit",cNameFile),"XLS")

IF cFilePath == ""
		*не вибрано
ELSE
	*вибрано
	TRY 
		F1.write(cFilePath,nType)
	CATCH TO oErr
		MESSAGEBOX("Файл уже використовується!"+CHR(13)+"Завершіть роботу з ним і повторіть експорт"+CHR(13)+"або змініть ім'я.",0+64,"Увага!")		
	FINALLY  
		
	ENDTRY

*!*

LOCAL cFilePath, nType, oFile, cNameFile

LOCAL oErr &&AS Exception


cFilePath = ""
nType = 11
cNameFile = ""
	LOCAL F1, namef
	DO CASE
	CASE thisform.pf.ActivePage = 1
		F1 = thisform.PF.Page1.F1_1
	CASE thisform.pf.ActivePage = 2
		F1 = thisform.PF.Page5.F1_55
		cNameFile = thisform.PF.Page2.Caption 
	CASE thisform.pf.ActivePage = 3
		F1 = thisform.PF.Page3.F1_3
		cNameFile = thisform.PF.Page3.Caption 
	CASE thisform.pf.ActivePage = 4
		F1 = thisform.PF.Page4.F1_4
		cNameFile = thisform.PF.Page4.Caption 
	CASE thisform.pf.ActivePage = 5
		F1 = thisform.PF.Page5.F1_55
		cNameFile = thisform.PF.Page5.Caption 
				
	OTHERWISE

	ENDCASE

cFilePath = PUTFILE("Збереження звіту...",IIF(EMPTY(cNameFile),"Zvit",cNameFile),"XLS")

IF cFilePath == ""
		*не вибрано
ELSE
	*вибрано

		F1.write(cFilePath,nType)
	CATCH TO oErr
		MESSAGEBOX("Файл уже використовується!"+CHR(13)+"Завершіть роботу з ним і повторіть експорт"+CHR(13)+"або змініть ім'я.",0+64,"Увага!")		
	FINALLY  
		
	ENDTRY

*!*		
*!*		ELSE 
*!*		
*!*			*IF thisform.check1.Value = 1
*!*					****Запускаємо ексель чи опен офіс

*!*					thisform.export(cFilePath)
*!*					thisform.LockScreen = .F. 				
*!*			*ENDIF 
*!*				
*!*		ENDIF 	
ENDIF 

ENDPROC

===== PROCEDURE loaddata =====
PROCEDURE loaddata
LOCAL __datenfrom,lnresult,lni
m.__datenfrom=datetime(thisform._yy1_,thisform._MM1_,1,0,0,0)

LOCAL R161GA,R161GB,R1611GA,R1614GA
STORE 0 TO m.R161GA,m.R161GB,m.R1611GA,m.R1614GA

LOCAL R81GA,R81GB,R811GA
STORE 0 TO m.R81GA,m.R81GB,m.R811GA

LOCAL R10GA,R10GB,R22GA,R21GA,R50GA
STORE 0 TO m.R10GA,m.R10GB,m.R22GA,m.R21GA,m.R50GA

LOCAL R101GA,R101GB,R102GA
store 0 TO m.R101GA,m.R101GB,m.R102GA

do dgr_box with 2, 'Триває завантаження розшифровки рахунку зачекайте 30 секунд','...'
*m.lnresult=prSQLEXEC(@hConnect,'exec Buh.dbo.us_Buh_Zvit_Tovar '+all2sqlstr(m.__FFCodeFirm)+',29,0,'+all2sqlstr(m.__datenfrom),'zn_zvit_tmp')
*MESSAGEBOX('exec Buh.dbo.us_Buh_Zvit_Tovar29 '+all2sqlstr(m.__FFCodeFirm)+','+all2sqlstr(m.__datenfrom))
m.lnresult=prSQLEXEC(@hConnect,'exec Buh.dbo.us_Buh_Zvit_Tovar29_202102 '+all2sqlstr(m.__FFCodeFirm)+','+all2sqlstr(m.__datenfrom),'zn_zvit_tmp')
do dgr_mov
if m.lnresult>=0
	if !used('zn_zvit_tmp') OR !used('zn_zvit_tmp4')
		MESSAGEBOX("Сервер у завантаженні відмовив!"+chr(13)+;
			"Поновіть дані з сервера!",0 + 16 + 0,'Сервер')
		do dgr_end
		thisform.release
		return
	ENDIF
ENDIF
do dgr_end
*zn_zvit_tmp список всіх додатків 2 до Розхідної накладної
*після 2010 року сюди попадають документи тільки БЕЗ двох галочок,
*потім додатки 2 а також ті звичайні податкові розхідні накладні з одною першою галочкою
*and not(F.isownerror=1 and F.isutochpdv=1)
*and (F.type=2 or 
*	(F.type=1 and F.isownerror=1 and F.isutochpdv=0))
*zn_zvit_tmp1 Список додатків до податкових прихідних накладних
*	після 2010 року сюди попадають всі документи без двох галочок

PROCEDURE xmlpart
LPARAMETERS key1,key2
LOCAL key1end,_xml,CRLF,_pids
CRLF = Chr(13)+Chr(10) 
m.key1end=key1+'END'
_xml=''
F1Find = thisform.PF.Page5.F1_55.DefineSearch(key1,1,1,49,thisform.PF.Page5.F1_55.maxRow,52,0)
	IF (F1Find.FindNext())
		IF thisform.PF.Page5.F1_55.RowHidden(F1Find.Row) OR VAL(thisform.PF.Page5.F1_55.TextRC(F1Find.Row,1)) = 1
			T1Start = F1Find.Row + IIF(thisform.PF.Page5.F1_55.RowHidden(F1Find.Row), 1, 0)
			F1Find = thisform.PF.Page5.F1_55.DefineSearch(key1end,1,1,49,thisform.PF.Page5.F1_55.maxRow,52,0)

ZVIT_BUH29_1_EDIT202102Command5commandbuttoncommandbuttonNPROCEDURE Click
LOCAL cFilePath, nType, oFile, cNameFile

LOCAL oErr &&AS Exception


cFilePath = ""
nType = 11
cNameFile = ""
	LOCAL F1, namef
	DO CASE
	CASE thisform.pf.ActivePage = 1
		F1 = thisform.PF.Page1.F1_1
	CASE thisform.pf.ActivePage = 2
		F1 = thisform.PF.Page5.F1_55
		cNameFile = thisform.PF.Page2.Caption 
	CASE thisform.pf.ActivePage = 3
		F1 = thisform.PF.Page3.F1_3
		cNameFile = thisform.PF.Page3.Caption 
	CASE thisform.pf.ActivePage = 4
		F1 = thisform.PF.Page4.F1_4
		cNameFile = thisform.PF.Page4.Caption 
	CASE thisform.pf.ActivePage = 5
		F1 = thisform.PF.Page5.F1_55
		cNameFile = thisform.PF.Page5.Caption 
				
	OTHERWISE

	ENDCASE

cFilePath = PUTFILE("Збереження звіту...",IIF(EMPTY(cNameFile),"Zvit",cNameFile),"XLS")

IF cFilePath == ""
		*не вибрано
ELSE
	*вибрано
	TRY 
		F1.write(cFilePath,nType)
	CATCH TO oErr
		MESSAGEBOX("Файл уже використовується!"+CHR(13)+"Завершіть роботу з ним і повторіть експорт"+CHR(13)+"або змініть ім'я.",0+64,"Увага!")		
	FINALLY  
		
	ENDTRY 
	
*!*		IF TYPE("oErr")="O"
*!*		
*!*		ELSE 
*!*		
*!*			*IF thisform.check1.Value = 1
*!*					****Запускаємо ексель чи опен офіс
*!*					thisform.LockScreen = .T. 
*!*					thisform.export(cFilePath)
*!*					thisform.LockScreen = .F. 				
*!*			*ENDIF 
*!*				
*!*		ENDIF 	
ENDIF 

ENDPROC
